    Saurin kawar da wancan tunanin tay tana tofe ko ina nata da addaan bacci. 
A hankali Moh Ameern ya rufe ƙofar ya mayar sam babu ƙarar takun sa ko kaɗan akan floor ɗin, shi yasa kuma bata lura ya ƙaraso har gaban gadon ba kasancewar ta riga da ta kashe bals, bata rufe bedroom nata sauda dama amma kuma saran shigo mata room Kai tsaye da Moh Ameern ɗin ya kowa ne ya tilasta fara rufe room nata. Na sani ba sekin sake maimaita min ba Meesherh Sameer Jadeens  tana dakon soyayyar wanda yake mantawa  da itama a tashi rayuwan, nasan duk waɗan nan abubuwan da kike yi pretend ne kawai kike a gaban mutane sedai ki sani rainin hankalinki  samm ba zai yi tasiri a gareni ba, saboda ina son ki tuna da cewan tare kike da Moh Ameern Saif Jedaans wanda ya gama sanin sirrinki tsafff don haka care your manner take a good calamity"  yay maganan da raɓata yana komawa cikin car nashi fuskansa ɗauke da Murmushin cimma nasarar shi gareta.
      A hankali cike da jin ciwon abinda ya aikata mata haka taci gaba da ratsa cikin company'n, cikin yanayin nutsuwan data zame mata dabi'a,tana takawa a hankali ƙamshinta da kuma yadda shigar ta amsheta kaɗai  na sake  jan hankulan jama'arn dake cikin company'n.       
        Yadda take ɗaukan hankulansu haka kwarjininta ke sake baibaye masu kallon nata, gashi dai a zahirance dai ita ce Wannan shahrriyar jarumar da kullum suke kallonta a poster ko a photo ko video, sedai yau da sukai ido biyu da ita duk se  wasu da suke jin suna da kwarin gwiwan mata magana da  haɗa ido da ita harma da tarar ta se kowa ya gaza yin hakan. Saboda yadda tsananin kwarjinin ta ya riga da ya gama baybayesu da ɗi da ƙari yadda fuskanta take a tamke. Tamkar irin kunun ƙanwar nan da aka dama ba'a haɗa shi ba haka fuskanta yake a wannan loƙacin.
      Tun daga nesa ta hangi Muhsin Aliey Yahaya zaune cikin tanfatsesiyar car nashi, ya zubo kafafunsa woje hannunsa riƙe da kwalbar da ta tabbatar da cewan wine ce. Sosai taji ranta ya saƙe ɓaci a karo na biyu tana jin mi yasa abubuwa da yawa ke son yawo da kuma wajiga rayuwanta haka?. Tamkar bata san da wanzuwarsa a wurin ba haka  tazo ta gitta shi ta fice abinta. Sedai shi ɗin ma ko taku cikakken uku batay da gitta shin ba yasha gabanta. Tun daga sama har ƙasa ya shiga binta da fitinannun shegun idanunsa haka harya sauƙe daman ƙirjinta da kuma cikinta dake lafe cikin shigan riga da wandon dake jikinta. Wani shegen numfashi yaje a sanda ya shaƙi ƙamshin mayen tularenta da yake bala'i bala'in ƙauna kamar me, cikin dakakken sautin amsa amo da kuma kuwwa ta furta. "Muhsin Aliey bani hanya na ce!!!" Ja baya yay kafin a hankali ya fara motsi da jajayen laɓɓansa, cikin muryan da ya fara tafiya da maye yake furta. "Meesherh kince na baki loƙaci na baki, kince na kaucewa ganinki a ko ina na kauce kince kince kince abubuwa da ya wa duk nabiatakan amma kuma loƙacin da kika ɗibar min sun cika kuma kinƙi bani damar taya kike tunanin zan baki amsa ba tare da kin amshi tayin soyayyar da nake mahaukacin yi miki ba!" Kanta ta kawar gefe zuciyanta na tafar fasa kamar me  don wani loƙacin ita kanta bata san yadda ake take gaza sarrafa fushinta akan koma waye ba, kamar few minutes ɗin nan data ɗauke fuskan Moh da zazzafan marinta da babu wasa. Babu zata haka ta fisgen kwalbar wine ɗin dake riƙe a hannun sa tana buga mai a fuska tana raɓa gefen sa ta wuce bayan tama security ɗin umarnin su fitar dashi a company'n kar kuma su bar kome kama dashi ya sake shigowa. "Meesherh don nace ina sonki shine har kike jin kina da kwarin gwiwan wulaƙanta Ni Muhsin Aliey Yahaya jarmai, to wllh kin yi kuskure mafi muni a cikin rayuwankin kan wulaƙanta nin da kikai" daga haka yay wurgi da securities ɗin ya faɗa Motarsa yana ɗibarta da gudun hauka ya fice gaba ɗaya daga harabar company'n.

  ***Daga Inda yake tsayen yay wani kalan Runtse  fararen idanunsa da  suka  masifar kaɗewa irin mai ban tsoro ɗin. Yana jin zuciyarsa tamkar zata ɓallo ko ta faso rufin fata bakwai ɗin dake ƙirjin ta fito. Wani hucin numfashin azaba ya fesar yana soma taka stairs ɗin bibbiyu yana yowa ƙasa. 
     Tana isa babban office nata daya ƙawatu sosai da kayan mo re rayuwa takai zaune saman cough ɗin dake gaban table editing  chiers ɗin, tare da  zube manyan wayoyinta tare da Lugagge bag nata. Ijiyar zuciya me ƙarfi ta sauƙe tana me tallafar kanta da duka hannuwanta biyun, Mayataccen ƙamshin da taji ya soma shiga hancinta ne ya sanya ta  Cikin nutsuwarta da rashin rawarn kai ta ɗago idanunta kaɗan tana bin ƙofan shigowa office ɗin  da kallo, dan wannan shegen unique ƙamshine da baya taɓa ɓacewa daga cikin rai da tunaninta. Daidai ganinta na sauƙa daga  inda yake tsaye jingine da bayan ƙofarn. ƙirjinta taji  yay wani irin motsawan tsoron biyo bayan tan da yay. Dan a Jinin jikinta tasan cewan dama yana cikin company'n. Sosai taji ƙirjinta na amsan dakan uku ukun da yake mata, zuba masa idonta tay tana masa kallo irin na  mamaki ko ruɗani ma zamuce ne oho. Amma kasancewarnta mutum bame barin ko wanne irin hali take bayyana bane ya sanyata majewa. fuskarnta na  a daken alamun babu  wani shock da taji ta tsinta kanta a ciki. Ya sani ze kuma iya rantse yafi kowanne mutum na cikin duniyar sanin halinta ciki da bai kasancewanta renonshi ce.Nunawa tay bata damu da yadda ya tsareta da idon ba se ma  janye idanun ta da tay  slowly tana  maidawa gefe daban.
         Gently shima ya lumshe nashi fararen  idanuwan san da kalan ɓacin ransu ke a bayyane. Ahankali ya shiga motsawa gabanta hannuwansa biyu ya sanya yana dafe table editing chiers ɗin dake gabanta. Se da ya ɗauka sama da 5 minutes tamkar me rowan yin maganan, kafin kuma cikin ɗan kaushi kaushin murya ya furta. "Babyyy!!!" Se kuma ya ga za ci gaba da maganar da yay niyan faɗin mata. Wani irin bugawa gabanta yay, a raunace ta ɗago da kanta tana zuba idanuwanta cikin ysakiyar nashi idanun. A tare kuma kamar an tunasar dasu wani abu suka janye daga kallon junansu. Ƙasa tay da kanta tanajin gabanta naci gaba da bugun da yake mata, Soft fararen zara zaran yatsun hannunsa uku ya sanya yana me ɗago fuskan nata.★ Lumshe idanunshi yay yana sake  shaƙar sassanyar iskar dake kaɗawa me garwaye da daɗdan ƙamshinta da yake shigan masa hanci. har zuwa lokacin kuma shi be janye idanun nasa a kanta ba 
 Runtse idanunta a karo na biyu tai  tana ƙoƙarin kwatar numfashin ta dake barazana wa gangar jikinta.  danne dukkan abinda ke yunƙuro mata a kan shi tay. A natse lips inta har na ɗan rawa a ƙoƙarin ta na ganin ta ambaci kalmar Pa'apah kaɗai. Sedai laɓɓantan sun mata nauyi sun gaza bata haɗin kai wurin yin maganan tan yadda ya kamata.  
        Lips natan dake motsa ya zuba idanunsa har adams apple ɗinsa na ɗan motsawa a maƙoshinsa."Me yasa Babyn pa'apah bata jin magana kamar yadda  ta dena jin tausayinsa" A tsanake yay maganan  Slowly yana janye idanunsa daga kallon lips. 
    A hankali a hankali yake yin baya baya yana ƙoƙarin danne abinda ke yunƙuro masa game da Babyn tashi. ƴar kuma rikicin sa Meesherh, da idanunta da suka  fara ƙyalli alamar taruwar hawayen da suka tara.tana jin yadda jinin jikinta ke wani irin harmutsawa tamkar ana aza wuta a jinin jikintan haka take ji, da idon taci gaba da binsa tana jin wani abu me kama da bijirewa kowa da zuciyarta akansa na ƙoƙarin ingiza ɗan ƙaramin lissafin ta. Yana fita ruwan da suka cika idanunta na zubowa da taji bala'i bala'in zafi da ƙunar da suka haifar mata. "I hate that bai ci ace zuciyana kinci gaba da yaudarana akan shi ba, and I LOVE YOU pa'apah dika nake son jin tsanar ka da kuma ƙaunarka, Inson jin tsanarka kan ƙoƙarin shata min layi da kuma buri na da kai ƙoƙarin yi tun a baya, ina kuma ci gaba da son jin S0 da kuma ƘAUNARKA a matsayin ka na pa'apah Naaaaa dikaaaa!!!"
      Haka dai ta shiga surkullen maganganun ta tana yin fatali da duk wani abu me amfani dake cikin babban office ɗin nata. A haka seda ta gama hargitsa komai, Babban P'A ɗin ta Tanim teddy  ne ya shigo da shirin faɗin mata ta fito ya tarar da ita cikin wannan halin. A take ya ƙira  Iklima wata ma'aikaciyace itama a cikin company'n, itane tay ƙoƙarin dakatar da Meesherh ɗin shi kuma ya shiga tattare komai yana killacewa wuri guda, marassa amfanin wanda suka tashi a aiki yana fita dasu. Tun a take yay ordering daga ɗayan company'n suka zo suka sake docoret na office ɗin. Zaman meeting ɗin da ba'ai ba kenan a ranar Iklimar ce ma ta taimaka mata wajen dawo da ita gida.
      Kasancewar da night  bayan sun dawo a sallah al'adanshi ne se yaje yayo sabon wanka ne yake sanya shi zuwa dining room din a ƙurarren loƙaci kullum.Fararen riga da wando na fendi  masu azabar laushii da kwanciya a  jikin mutum ne a jikinsa. Fararen sawayensa kuma suna sanye cikin Blue Lorenzo's slippers masu taushi, ga wannan mayen ƙamshinsa dake rige rigen kaima hancinansu ziyara tun kafin isowansa parlour'n. 
  Wani irin tsalle taji zuciyarta yayi a karo na biyu,iyaka ƙamshinsa kaɗai karyar mata da zuciya yake, se dai kwarai da gaske tana ƙoƙari wajen shanye hakan,Cikin irin jarumtar da take da ne ya sanya kallo ɗaya ta masa ta ɗauke kanta tana ci gaba da sipping coffee creamer ɗin ta. A cikin zuciyarta ayyanawa take ehhhhhh lallai Pa'apanta na daban ne kuma  me kyau ne da kuma tsafta. Wanda indai magana ake ta wanka yabi jiki to kuwa ba tantama tasan pa'apanta ƙarshe ne.  Sam babu ko ɗigon annuri a kyakykyawan farar fuskan shi haka babu wasa ko alamarsa a saman fuskarsa nashi da tai wani kalan  fayau, wanda kai tsaye ita tasan dalilin daya haddasa may hakan.
   Don kamar yadda yasan duka halayensa haka itama ta san duka halayensa da kuma kalan damuwan da yake shiga ko akasin hakan. Kowa dake parlour'n wani kalan shiru yay har ya  gama saƙƙowa,kallon kowa baiyi a cikin parlour'n ba saima ta bayan parlour'n daya zaga yana shigewa cikin balcony in da kai tsaye nan ake jera may abu mara nauyi duk daren duniya ba tare da su yake yin lonch ba. Wayane maƙale a kunnensa wanda da dukkan alamu wata wayace yake yi muhimmanci.
    yana yin wayar ne kuma hannunsa na ɗauke da wani haɗaɗɗen glass  cup mai ɗauke da Whipped cream a ciki yana sha.    ★ Bayan ya kammala woyan kai tsaye ya maida kallonsa ga Mohan ɗin. "Yaya abubuwa sosae suke yin kyau game da binciken sirrin da muke yi kamar yadda ka buƙatan ama. To Alhamdulillh se dai mu ce Masha Allah" Gefin lips nashi ya ɗan ciza kaɗan se kuma ya furta "don't worry zamu magana daga baya ina buƙatan hutawa yanzu" kansa ya gyaɗa may yana miƙewa harya fara tafiya kuma ya tsinkayi muryan babban yayan nasu yana ambatar sunan shi kai tsaye da *Mohan Sameer* chakk ya tsaye  tare da waigowa alamar gaskata in ƙiran ne ya koma. nodding Kansa yay alaman ehhhhhh ƙiran yay, baya ya dawo yana zama inda ya tashi. Duk yawan tarin dukiyar da kake da to fa idan wani ze ciri wani abu ya baka as matsayin kyautatawa wani kalan jin daɗi kake.  Kwarai kyauta Rahma ce kuma takan sanya mutum yaji daɗi da shauƙin kyautan da aka masa. Godiya ya may cike da fara'a yana barin wurin gaba ɗaya don bashi space ɗin kamar yadda ya buƙata.

     A ƙalla seda ya debi wasu ƴan lukuta masu tsayi kana ya miƙe yana bin  ƙofan fita wajen parlour'n ta baya. 
     Ji yay a jikinsa tamkar ana kallonsa loƙacin da yake tafiya domin nufar ɓangaren sa, tana tsaye cikin kayan shan iska amma ba masu nuna tsiracin mutum ba kanta babu cap hannunta riƙe da woya tana daddannawa. Ido biyu sukai tay saurin janye nata tamkar dama can ba shi take kallo. Se hakan ya Hasko wani abu daga cikin  memory'n baya cann. Ko me ya tuno kuma oho amma kome ya tuni me girma ne dan wani kalan tattausan Murmushin gefin baki ya saki yana wucewar sa kai tsaye.     Bayan ya fito ɗaure da alwulan da al'adan gidan ne ba'a kwanciya ba tare da alwulan ba, sedai wasu sukan yi wasu kuma basu yi haka nan tsarin yake. Ko kafin ya fito tuni Mah ta sake gyare bed ɗin  daga nan suka kwanta abinsu se bacci.

Ance wai dare mahutar bawa, kamar ko wanne rai me numfashi ke kwance domin samun hutu ga rayuwarsa cikin kwanciyar hankali yana barcinsa, Se dai a dai-dai wannan loƙacin Sheikh juraij thabit ke zaune saman darduma yana rera karatun Al- Qur'ani mai girma,Cikin wani irin amo  madaidaiciyar muryarsa mai daɗin sauraro da sanya ma zuciyoyi nutsuwa yake raira karatun.    Sanya hannun sa masu taushi yay yana kama na sheik ɗin cikin nashi, bakin bed ɗin Sheikh ɗin  Abba ya zaunar dashi shima yana zama kusa dashi. Se daya ɗan ja wasu second haka kafin cike da tsananin tausayin yaron nashi ya furta.
"JURAIJ akwai wata alfarma da nake nema awajenka!" Cike da  wani Irin baƙon yanayin da zuciya ke ɗauka a bazata  ya ɗago kansa yana kallon Abban cike da girmamawa ya furta.
"Abba kawai kace wani  Umarni zaka bani amma ba alfarma zaka nema daga gareni  ba. Saboda kafi ƙarfin neman alfarma awajena sai dai ka bani Umarninka kawai, Ni kuma a koda yaushe me biyayya ne ga dukkanin  umarninka"

   Zama da kyau Abba ya gyara kana ya fuskancesa da kyau cikin raunin zuciya da amincin da yakeji akan Sheikh juraij ɗin yace.
 "Um-um  Juraij alfarmar dai kam nake nema" Cikin jin nauyin mahaifin nashi da ganin kimansa ya sake risinar da kansa ƙasa, muryan sa har na shirin karyewan raunin da tay ya furta. Don sosai Maganan Anniey ya buge shi se dai samm baya taɓa damuwan komi akan face nashi kan duk wani mahluki dake cikin estate ɗin nasu koma na ce family.
 Jin rauni ya wanzu a cikin muryan papiey ne ya sanya ta itama  ɗin sake kame hannuwansa cikin nata ta na kasa cewa komai dan tasan kuma ba zai iya ci gaba da maganan ba kenan. 
         Ɗan lumshe fararen idanuwanta tayi A bayyane kuma ta shiga furta adda'ar "Rabbi inni lima anzalta ilayya min khairin faƙir³ Ra rabbi inni magalubun fantasir³  la haula wala quwwati illa billahil aliyal azeem³, Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihi raji'un ³" haka ta shiga ambata tare da sauran adda'o'in da duk suka zo cikin bakinta. Ajiyar zuciya me sanyi papiey ɗin ya sauƙe a natse yanajin hankalinsa na dawowa jikinsa take yaji dacin zuciyarsa ya ragu sbd yadda adda'o'in data karanto ɗin suka ratsa shi sosai tare da zuciyarsa. 
slowly kuma ta shiga ware idanunta  tare da sake sanya hannunta tana jinginar da kanta jikin papiey ɗin nata, daga haka tafiyan nasu ya koma na kurame har suka ƙara so mahaukacin estate ɗin su, Shi ya fara fita jikinsa duk a wani kalan amma yadda yake tafiyan cikin nutsuwa kai kace babu abinda ke damun Rayuwar sa nan. Lugagge bag na papiey kawai ta dauke se handbag nata tana bin bayan mahaifin nata. tana tafiyar ne kuma tana ɗan  shedding tears nata zuciyan ta na ƙara yin nauyin abubuwa da yawa da suka kanainaye cikin rayuwarta.
   A bakin  entry na babban  parlour'n gidan sukayi kiciɓus da Moh Ameern Saif inda ita ta tura shi kuma ya turo. Kin sakin ƙofan tayi tana kuma ƙin kallonsa, sake  murɗa handle door ɗin yay  yana jan ƙofar baya ya kuma ja ya tsaye yana ƙare mata kallo tun daga samanta zuwa ƙasa. A ɗan kausashe ta furta "Moh Bani hanya" 
      Ɗan girgiza kai  yayi kawai tare da ɗan taunar lisp nashi, Wani shegen killer smile yayi tare da ɗan shafar gashin ƙeyarsa yana me furta "Ohh Ohhh beb wai cikin sauƙi haka?"  Yay tambayan cikin ɗan iskan salon jan hankali da kuma rashin mutumci. Chakkk ta tsaya daga ƙoƙarin raɓa gefen Moh Ameern ɗin da take niyar yi. Sakamakon tausasan hannuwan da taji ya raba tsakanin su ta hanyar janye ta daga niyar shigan, ko bata juyo ba ZUCIYAR TA ta riga da  ta gama sanar mata ko wa ne ne. Hakan yasa ta jan idanunta ta lumshe su gaba ɗaya. Moh Ameern daya ƙame cike da tsoro da kuma kafe Sheikh juraij ɗin da ido yake kallonsa, Shima idanuwansa da suke a rufen ya buɗe tarrr akan Moh Ameern. Kafin ya saki wani murmushin rainin wayo tare da zamar da ƙasan lips nashi ya furta.  "Shi kuma wannan ko a cikin wanne hadisin ne ko sura ohooooo, amma ko ba komi abin ya bada gitta da ƙyau Sheikh babban salo!!!" 
 Haka muryan juraij ɗin ya dake shi Sheikh juraij ɗin. A natse ba tare da garajen komi ba ya rabata da jikinsa yana jefawa Moh Ameern ɗin wani kalan  mugun kallo me tattare da cikakken gargaɗi. Ita ne ta fara barin wurin tana jin yadda ƙirjinta ya ɗan ƙare mata tare da mata wani kalan nauyi wanda dama har yanzun bawai ta gama warkewa bane rass.
    ★A ƙaton parlour'n su  wanda yake ɗauke da duk wani nau'in jin daɗin rayuwa ta tarar da duka su Maamah har dama ƙarin babbar yayarsu ummeterh. Da sauri ummeterh ta nufo ta tare da  riƙe hannunta fuskanta  ɗauke da kyakykyawan Murmushin da ya sake fidda tsananin kamanninsu kasancewar su duka Ukun kamanninsu ɗaya. "Ma sha ALLAH Dear lallai jiki yayi sauƙi tunda har aka iya fita shooting yau" ɗan murmushin yaƙe tayi tana shafa kan baby'n nuwaira da  ita ma ta nufo su fuskanta cike da farin cikin ganin Aunty Meesherh ɗin.    "To banda abinki Mamman'su  Karfa ki manta Meesherh fa ita ma ce ce wata rana haihuwa zatay, tun daga yanxun idan bata koyi ƙarfin hali ba se yaushe kike son ta koya?" Papah ya bata amsa da hakan yana hayewansa sama. Mohan Sameer da shigowansa kenan parlour'n shima yama kansa mazauni  akan ɗaya daga cikin expensive chairs da suka ma parlour'n ƙawanya. Yana ɗora ƙafarsa ɗaya kan ɗaya yana fuskantansu su duka cike da farin cikin ganinsu su dukansu. Aunty Ummeterh ya fara gaisarwa kana ya sake gaida Maamah duk da kuwa cewan babu jimawa suka Hausa ɗin. Lil juraij dake riƙe da hannun Mohan ɗin ne ya zame cikin wayonsu irin na yara kai tsaye ya nufi inda Meesherh take, duk kuwa da yadda take ƙoƙarin ɗauke fuska daga yaron,cikin muryan gyaranci ba sosai ba ya ce "Otty kin ga ni paap ne ya bani kuma yace karna fatawa kowa ma" yay maganan cikin gwaranci ba sosai yana mata nuna da wani Rolex watch na diamond gold me kyau kalan na yaran nan me shegen tsada da kyau. Ɗan taɓe baki Maamah tayi tana juyar da kanta daga kallon yaron tana maidawa kansu Ummeterh da Mohan da suke wata maganar  daban.
     Miƙewa tayi da niyar tafiya amma lil juraij ya maƙale mata duk yacce ta so data yagar dashi kuwa. Gashi zuwa loƙacin kanta sosai taji yana juya mata gashi wasu masallatan harsun fara ƙiran sallah, dole dashi ta tafi sama yana maƙale da ita. Duk da ido suka bita kafin Mohan ya kalla Maamah tare da furta "Maamah  don ALLAH Meesherh ta dakata da aikin nata  don yanzu kamata yay ace tana gidan aurenta, Ki duba fa tana age na 24 year's ba ai yaci ace ma tana da nata babay'n itama" tunda ya soma maganar Maamah ta kafe shi da ido ko ƙiftawa batayi seda ta tabbar da ya gama ta furta. 
       "To sannu ubana da wannan ai kamata kawai yay ka bani umarni, ka ga Mohan ka fita ido na In rufe idan har kaima ba zaka iya bin Meesherh da fatan Alkhairi ba to kamata yayi kaima ka janye idanuwanka daga gare ta" ta ƙarasa maganan a fusace tana miƙewa tare da barin parlour'n baki ɗaya, don Maamah irin iyayen nan ne masu shegen S0n ƴaƴansu wanda basu ganin laifinsu ko kaɗan, se dai sammm bata barin ko waye daga cikin yaran nata ya aikata ba daidai ba kuma ita mutum ce da bata iya ƙunbiya ƙunbiya da abu ba ita ɗin karan tsaye ce ƴar kai tsaye me gaɓa². Ko waye ya taka ta to kuwa lallai itama zata taka shi don bata ɗaukar raini kamar yacce itama bata raina kowa se har in mutum shi ne ya fara shiga hurumin ta da wata sigar ta daban, to kuwa lallai anan ne zaka gane ko kai ɗin wane ne.
  Ajiyar zuciya me tattare da damuwa Ummeterh da Mohan Sameer suka sauƙe kusan loƙaci guda kan kalaman mahaifiyar nasu, duk kuwa da cewan ba yau bane suka saba fuskantar hakan daga gareta. kawai abinda suka sani suke kuma so shi ne suga sun  inganta rayuwar Autar nasu, sun gaji da ce ce kucen da ake akan Meesherh ɗin daga media harma da nan cikin gidan nasu kuwa. *Sheikh juraij* 
  Tunda yabar wurin kai tsaye cikin garden  yay shigewansa, sanyi da kuma iskan dake kaɗawa a wurin na  ratsashi wanda har  yake jin kamar fatar jikinsa ce ta bude kai tsaye sanyin yake ratsa masa ko wanne sassa na jikinshi  yana daskarar da duk wata magudanar  jini ta jikinsa, kamar kullum yana sanye cikin shigar farin yadi me azabar laushi da tsada ɗinkin jumper cables irin na zamanin nan amma kuma ba masu sherp da yawa ba irin na matasan yanzu. Sumar nan tasha gyara na musamman se fidda daɗdan ƙamshin take yi gami da daukar ido. Sajen nan yay luf luf abunsa har wani kalan  shine blue yake bayarwa daga nesa.   
   Sanyi haɗi da daɗin iskar ne ya sanya shi kasa zama wuri guda don haka ya shiga zagaye cikin garden ɗin,Hannuwansa goye a bayansa daga can ƙasan zuciyarsa kuwa yake furta. 
   _Subhanallah,wal hamdu lillah, wala'ila ha ilallahu wallahu akhbar³_ 

Da dai duk sauran adda'o'in da duk suka zo bakinsa, kai tsaye ba zaka taɓa fuskantan tasbihi yake ba sedai ka kalla fatar lips nashi kaɗan tana motsawa. Sammm yama rigada ya manta da abinda ya farun yanzu ko na ce dazu. Ganin loƙacin ƙiran sallah yayi ne ya sanya shi fita, se da ya fara shiga bedroom nashi kana ya fito kai tsaye yana shigewa cikin babban masallacin dake cikin anguwarsu. Shi ne ya ƙira sallar ya kuma ja jami'i cikin wannan daddaɗan sautin nashi da sauraran yanda yake fidda tajweed nashi  yake sanya mutane cika masallacin duk kuwa kalan girman da yake da. 
      Dama shi ka'idan sane baya shigowa se bayan sallar Isha'i sosai shi yasa kowa na gidan ke riga shi kammaluwa a dining room ɗin,duk da kasancewar tonight baya zaman dining room ɗin amma kuma hakan jiran shigowarn nashi ya zama kamar  al'adan kowa.
     Iyayensu mata kawai ya gaida kasancewar sun gaisa da iyayensu maza tun a can cikin masjid ɗin.Nan ƙannashi maza da matan suka gaida shi shima,A natsensa ya amsa su yana kallar bakin Meesherh da kuma Moh Ameern Saif da babu su a cikin jadawalin gaisuwar.
  Directly balcony ɗin da ya saba zama kai tsaye wuce maids ɗin dake aikin shirya dinner ɗin na binsa da tea plask ɗin dake ɗauke da zazzafan tea wanda duk daren duniya shi ne kawai yake sha. Jere mishi tay daga inda yake idanunsa lumshe yana ambatar sunan ALLAH acan ƙasan ranshi. Se da ya tabbatar ta bar wurin kana ya buɗe idon yana jan tea plask gabansa. A natse yake sipping tea ɗin abinsa   daga gefe dashi kuma  System  nashi ce  yake aikin sarrafa ta. Yau be daɗe a wurin ba kamar kullum ya tashi ya tafi don yana da abinda zeyi a daren. Ga yawan bugun da zuciyarsa ke mai akai akai yana jin tamkar wani abu mara daɗi na dab da faruwa dashi, Amma sosai yay ƙoƙarin danne hakan se adda'o'in sa da yaci gaba dayi yana adda'an koma miye zai faru dashi to Ubangiji ya kawo shi cikin sauki ya kuma takai ta ma masa ɗin, da haka yanar wurin yana daura alwula tare da fara jero nafil fili wa mahalicci'nsa...
     
☆ Meesherh kuwa koda ta koma bedroom nata wanka tayo ta shirya kanta cikin singlet ta mata da wando. Army green da farar singlet  inda tayi stocking  singlet  ɗin cikin wandon ta ɗaure da beld. Sai light green ɗin  top wadda ta ɓalle duka maɓallanta ta barta a buɗe, Iskar da ake kaɗawa a gurin na diban rigar nata tana  kamar zata cire,gaba ɗaya rigar tabar jikinta sai singlet ɗin kawai, tsaye take daga jikin window na bedroom ɗin ta tayi crossing ƙafafunta  suna a harɗe. Sosae iskar dake shigowa  ta cikin window'n ɗakin ke mata daɗin shaƙa tare da ƙamshin flowering dake ata bayan window nan suke sake bata nishaɗi da jin cikakkiyar nutsuwa, saima taji ta nema ciwon kan data shigo dashi ta ne meshi ta rasa.♡ Shi yasa most especially Idan tana cikin yanayi mara daɗi idan bata son fita balcony takan tsaya a wurin ne ta yaye duka labulayen wurin nan take samun cikakken nishaɗi sosai.

  Lumshe fararen idanuwanta  tayi tare da faɗawa wata duniyar  ta daban, tunani take akan baya  idan tace baya tana tunanin Baya cann da dadewa kafin rugujewar komi. A cikin idanunta dake lumshe komai daya faru yake dawo mata daki daki tamkar komai daya faru ne a waɗan can loƙutan haka komai ya Shiga zeroying mind nata. Saurin dawowa hayyacinta tayi sakamon zuciyan ta da taji tana son maido mata da hannun agogo baya, Bayan nan da har gaban abada bata fata da burin dawowarta cikin rayuwarta koda a matsayin mahaifin ne a gareta. Domin mahaifiyarsa ta rigada ta gama ɓata komi tun a wancen loƙacin. A yadda ta tashi ta tashi ne ta buɗe Ido da shi, Shi ne mutum na farko data fara ƙira da pa'apahh...........
     Akan shi ta fara koyan magana tana yima kowa ƙyuwwa amma banda shi ciki kuwa har da Maamah'nta. Domin matukar Meesherh tana wurin juraij bata yarda da kowa bata kuma zuwa wurin kowa se pa'apahh'nta juraij. Ta kai ta kawo ko bacci zatay se da shi a gefenta, shi ke maga wanka ya wanke mata fufu idan tayi, ya goye ta a gadon bayansa da zani irin yanda uwa kewa ɗan ta, a loƙacin shi yana da shekaru sha bakwai a duniya ita kuma tana da 1 year's a cikin duniyar, Shi ne ya Renata yaci kashinta yaci fitsarinta har zuwa sanda ta shekara biyar a duniya, A kuma daidai loƙacinne  abubuwa suka soma Sanja kansu, suka kuma soma samun saɓani wanda shi ne farkon jinin tubalin da har ya kai  su ga irin wannan matakin da suke kai a yanzu.   ♡
     Duk wasu ɗabi'iu kalan nashi take yi masu kyau a da can baya kai takai ta kawo ko cima bata cin abinda baya ci, iyaka abinda pa'apahh'nta yake ci itama shi take ci. Saɓanin farko da suka fara samu a cikin rayuwarsu shi ne tun da ta ɗan ƙara wasu shekaru a cikin shida zuwa tara/goma ya fuskanta ita ɗin mayyar jin kiɗe-kiɗe da waƙe waƙe ce, ga shegen son kallon tsiya a wurin ta kamar me, duk yacce zeyi don ganin ya cire mata hakan abin yaci tu ra. Har yakai ga ya dena shiga lamuranta na wasu ƴan loƙuta haka zata zo ta shige jikinsa tayi ta masa koke koken banza har se ya dawo ya kula ta, Amma hakan bazai hana gobe ta sake kunna casunta ba, haka zata sa ata sawo mata irin kayan indiyawa sune kayan sawanta. Ya tattare ya ƙone  amma haka Maama zata sake sawo mata wasu.  Kai har takai ta kawo Meesherh ta dena shiga cikin duk wasu lamura na Juraij ta dena tsoronsa ta dena ƙiransa da sunan data bashi na pa'apahh wanda a da yayyenta shi kuma ƙannensa koda mutum ne yay kuskuren ƙiran sunan shi da sunan da ita kaɗai take son take ƙiransa da abinda to kuwa ranan wunin rigima zata wuni tana yi. Amma se gashi sannu a hankali komi nasu na sake samun saɓanin ra'ayi.

 "Maamah kaina kamar ze fashe ciwo ciwo ciwoooo yake min sosai!!!"  Saurin tarota jikinta Maamah ɗin tayi jikinta harna rawa wajen furta "Subhanallahi amma kuma shi ne kika kwana a haka? ☆༻ Hakan ya sanya cikin wani kalan sassarfar dashi kansa be san yana da itaba ya Isa ga car ɗin. Idanunsa na rarrabe masa be ma san sanda ya zare babban ƴar cikin rigarsa yay jifa da ita ba. Shi kansa ba zai iya cewa ga sanda ya isa ga motar ba abinda kawai ya sani shidai ya ganshi ɗauke da Meesherh ɗin cikin jini kuma bata numfashi, kana duk da kasancewan shi ba me gyaran karaya bane amma yana da tabbacin kusan duka sassan jikin ta a kareraye suke. Ko kallon gabansa bayyi taku kawai yake a farfajiyan filin wurin da mutanen suka zagaye. Idanunsa da farat ɗaya suka sanja ya ɗago yana watsawa wasu daga cikin tsirarin mutanen da suke faɗin kada ya taɓa ta ya sauƙe ta ya bari police suzo. Ko kafin ya isa ga motar'nshi tuni Personal person ɗin sa ya buɗe may motar. Yana ƙoƙarin shiga ya dakatar da shi ba tare daya furta masa ko ƙala ba.
     Da wani kalan bala'in gudu na fita hayyaci ya fige motar wanda harya tsorata Personal person ɗin sa Al'ameen Hafeez dama wasu daga gurin, tun da ya taka kan motar yake wani irin bata wuta na fita a hankali. Kai tsaye S Power  specialist hospital ya nufa, A wani kalan tashin hankali ya taka motar yana fitowa a tsiyace.
Cikin tafasar jini yake takunsa Meesherh ɗauke a hannayensa ganan jini se malali yake daga jikinta. Amma hakan sammm be dame shi duk nurse ɗin da yaci karo da ita tayi ƙoƙarin amsanta amma seya kauce ma hakan amma basa daddara haka wasun su ke sake nufo shi kasancewar sa sanannen mutum da gari dama duniyar ke wani irin karrama shi da girmama shi a matsayin sa na babban malami. 
   Shi kansa ba zai ce ga randa ya taɓa shiga tashin hankali kwatankwacin wannan ba.

  Ko cikakken 30 mints ba'ai ba zaratan matasan Jedaans Mention suka cika hospital din, Wanda Kai tsaye Personal person ɗin juraij ɗin ne ya ƙira thabit Jedaans da kuma Sameer Jedaans. Wanda kusan loƙaci guda Manyan mazajen suka shigo Hospital ɗin. Iyaka yanayin tsaron dake zagaye da Hospital ɗin kaɗai ya tada hankalin su ba kaɗan. Bare kuma azo kan yadda tun kamin ka shiga room ɗin da Meesherh ɗin ke kwance cikin hali na Rai ko mutuwa ƙara da bututun na'urorin dake tashi kaɗai tashin hankali ne. Hakan yayi dai da loƙacin da zuciyar prof Sameer Jedaans Turaki tay wani kyakkyawan bugu na tashin hankalin ganin a yadda yanayin Meesherh yake. ☆Wani irin numfashi Prof Sameer Jedaans ya fidda yana runtse idanuwanshi kamun kuma ya sake  buɗe ganinsa Ahankali tar akan Shalelen Rayuwarsa. Billionaire juraij thabit Jedaans kam daina motsi yayi se Idanuwanshi shi ma daya kafe Meesherh ɗin dashi amma baya fahimtar komai dake wakana a cikin duniyar.
      Mohan Sameer Jedaans da bai daɗe da shigowa bane  shima ya tsinta kansa da tsayawa wuri ɗaya chakkk. Don bai tsammaci abin yayi tsanani har haka ba duk kuwa da cewar babu da wanda yayi magana cikin su, Amman yanayinsu da yanda  sukai shiru ɗin kawai ya kalla ya gama karantar komi dake shirin faruwa dasu wato rasa Meeshel ɗin su, Rayuwar su. A take shima yaji  iskar wajen na canza masa da kalan tashin hankali. A natse ya miƙe yana barin accident & emergency room ɗin gaba ɗaya, daga yanayin yadda yake takunsa da cikakken ƙarfi na hasali zaka san cewar ehhhh lallai shi ɗin matashi  ne mai ji da lafiya kuma  cikakken namiji ne. Hannunsa ɗaya da yaji an kamane ya sanya shi tsayawa chakkk,juyowa yayi don ganin ko waye ganin Sameer Jedaans ne tsaye daga bayansa ya sanya shi risinar da kansa ƙasa sosai. Cikin muryan rauni sosai ya furta  "Ina kuma zakaje bayan ana buƙatan ganin mahaifi'n Meesherh?" Yana furta hakan ya saki hannun Sheikh juraij ɗin tare da komawa cikin room ɗin da ya fito. Wani irin daskarewa a wurin yayi yana rasa mi ma ya kamata yayi. Tamkar wanda ake ja d linzami haka ya koma inda ya fito ɗin kai tsaye ya shige Room ɗin da dactor ke son ganinsa. A natse ya tura babban office ɗin da yake ƙaton gaske tamkar parlour'n wata matar, da hannu Dr Anas Mansur Ma'aji yama Sheikh nunin ya zaune shima ya ɗan fara manyan ta don har ya kusan fin Sheikh ɗin ma a haife. Idanunsa ya janye akan Sheikh ɗin tare da tura may file ɗin dake ɗauke da sakamon gwajin Meesherh wanda Taɓo da lamba da kuma hatsari dake jikin Rubutun ne ya sanya shi kasa iya furta masa komi.sabida nauyi da kuma kunyarsa da yake ji me tsanani, Hannunsa na rawa zuciyarsa na wani kalan mahaukacin bugu ya ɗauka file ɗin. Tun daga layin farko daya Runtse fararen idanuwan sa be yarda ya sake buɗe suba. Su kuwa acan woje duk suma hankalinsu ba'a kwance ba ganin shirun Sheikh ɗin yayi yawa.
 
   A ƙalla su Sameer Jedaans sun kusan zaman kusan awa ɗaya zuwa biyu a wurin kuma har zuwa loƙacin basu ga fitowar Sheikh juraij ɗin ba daga cikin office ɗin. Hakan shike sake tada musu da hankali. Cikin tsananin ɗaci da damuwar zuciya ya fito ya fito daga ɗakin yana sauƙe numfashi me tsananin zafi zaune yakai akan kujeran da take kallan su daddy'n kafin ya ɗago idanuwansa kai tsaye yana sauƙe su akan Sameer Jedaans turaki.  
       ★Thabit Jedaans kam  kasa zama  yayi saboda kansa da yaji ya masa  wani kalan dummmm kafin nauyin hakan ya biyo baya ya danne. Shigowan Moh Ameern Saif sanye da riga da wando na bacci long sleeve daidai da miƙewa tsayen da Sameer Jedaans Turaki yayi a mugun kiɗime don shine ƙarshen duban file ɗin.      
       Idanuwansa da sukafi na kowannensu sauyawa ya ɗago ya kalli kowannensu kafin ya dawo da kallonsa kan Sheikh juraij thabit a cikin su yana neman wanda ze bashi kwarin gwiwa akan hakan. Tun kamin ya iya furta komai juraij ɗin yay hanzarin miƙewa  yana  ficewa zuwa ɗakin da Meesherh ɗin take, tana kwance sanɓal kai baka isa tantance a wanne condition take ba illa kawai abinda zuciyan ka zata iya Hasko maka  akanta wato Rigar kowa Mutuwa. 
 A natse thabit Jedaans da ya biyo bayan juraij ɗin ya sauƙe ijiyan zuciyan ɓoyayya ganin inda juraij ɗin ya nufa. Daidai kuma da shigowan Maah tare da ihun kururwan data buga na ganin halin da Meesherh'n take. Ita kanta da da farko bata gane ta don farko kamar gezau idanunta suka hasko mata ita. Tana kallan itane kuwa ta fasa wata uwar kururwan ƙara tare da zubewa ƙasa sumammiya itama, Shigowan Sameer Jedaans Kenan ya fahimci akwai wani abin kenan a kuma kwai abinda ba daidai yake tafiya ba.  Ajiyar zuciya da numfashi kawai ya sauƙe tare da kaiwa ƙasa daidai inda Maah take. Kowane ya fice a room ɗin ana barinsu su kaɗai se kuwa majinyaciyar dama bata san inda kanta yake a ciba. Da kyar ya samu ta kawo numfashi tana buɗar idanunta kuwa akan shi ta kife kanta a ƙirjinsa ta shiga riskar kuka na tsantsan tashin hankali.  Cikin kulawa da nuna tausayawa sosai da nutsuwa da kuma sanyi a cikin muryan'shi  ya furta. "Ba kuka ya kamaci ace kin tsaya zaman yiba Mamansu! Duk da nasan hakan da ciwo amma a wannan condition ɗin da Meesherh'n mu ke ciki adda'an mu da fatan Alkhairinmu kawai take da buƙata" daga haka ya miƙe jikinsa matuƙar sanyaye yana barin ɗakin. Farar takardar data faɗo daga jikinsa tay hanzarin kaiwa hannuwanta tana rarumarta. Bibbiyu idonta suka fara kallon ƙaddararren rubutun da yake tun daga rabbil izzati jalla wa'allah. Hakan ya sanya idanunta fara ganin dishi dishi sedai kasancewanta mutum me taurin Rai ya sanya taƙi aminta ta sake komawa duniyar sumewa a karo na biyu.
     Hakan ne yay sanadiyyar miƙewanta a razane tana fita kanta na jujjayawa la yi na kuma na ɗibarta tamkar ze yasar a ƙasa. Duk suna inda suke tsaitsaye  harda ƙarin wosu ma, Tana zuwa idanun ya rine yayi jajur ta shiga nuna ma  papiey takar cikin muryan  da ma sammm bata san da ma  wanne kalan yare daidai koba daidai ba ta shiga furta. "Papiey'n Meesherh?" Se kuma ta dakata tare da ɗago rinannun idanunta a karo na biyu ta ƙirayi sunan shi kai tsaye. "SAMEER!! Wannan file ɗin na wanene? Gashi dai ina kallon takardar sakkk me irin sunan Meesherl ɗin mu, 😳 amma sedai ita wannan wai naga ta samu 
 lack of memory (wato tabuwar ƙwaƙwalwa), kuma a jikin file ɗin naga kamar ansa Mahaifarta tayi damege ba zata iya haihuwa ko ɗaukan ciki bafa..... ALLAH Sarki baiwar Allah" 
     Ta yi maganar wasu hawayen da bata san da suba na zubo mata.  Da baya da baya kowa na dake wurin ya shiga ja, kowa najin zuciyarsa ba zata iya ɗaukan wannan babban lamarin dake tunkaro su ba. Hannuwan Ummeterh na rawa jiki sanyaye idanunta jawur ta sanya tana riƙo hannun mahaifiyarta dake ƙarƙashin sakin file ɗin ƙass. Saurin taro file ɗin tayi tare da haɗawa da hannun Maah ɗin tana jimƙesu wuri guda. "Maahhhhh wannan ba file bane da zaki saki a ƙass iska ta tafi dashi, daganan masu shara su shara su tafi dashi bola.... Wannan ɗin *GABA ƊAYA RAYUWAR  Ꮇ𝞔𝞔𝘚Η𝞔Ʀh   𝙎𝙖𝙢𝙚𝙚𝙧   𝕁𝔼ᴰᴬᴬᴺˢ ce wallafe a cikinta, wanda ƙaddara ta kaita ga zuwa wannan matakin!!!" Se kuma kuka ya sarƙe mata jikinta na rawa dama duk wanda basu san ainihin takamaiman abinda ke faruwa da Rayuwar Meesherh Jedaans ba. 
  Ɗagowa Maah ɗin  tayi tare da kallon Ummeterh ɗin wacce ta  gyaɗa mata kai alamar ehhhhhh lallai tabbacin maganar kenan. Amma duk da haka bata gasgata taba hakan ne ya sanya ta kwace hannunta a cikin na ummeterh tan tare da saurin riƙo na papiey. Cikin idanuwan sa ta jefa nata kwayar idanun can tsakiyar su, shima kan kawai ya iya ɗaga mata wasu hawayen da tun farko suka ƙi zuwar masa na saƙƙo masa. Hakan ne ya sanya bama tasan sanda ta zare hannunta cikin na papiey'n ba tana juyawa ga ummeterh'tan tare da sauƙe mata wani bahagon marin da ya ratsa zuciyar kowa dake wurin. 
   "Ashe dama ke ma bame hankalin bace Ummeterh? Me yasa sai kece wacce zata fara taryana da wannan mugun labarin. Ko dan kawai kunga ina ƙaunarku da yawa da yawa da yawa..... Amma ai duk da haka ban cancanta irin wannan wasan ba, naji Meesherh tayi  accident amma iyaka yau ɗaya ai baici ace duk  taji waɗan nan abubuwan ko?" Tai maganan kanta na tangal tangal ganinta kai tsaye na sauƙa akan juraij dake tsaye nesa dasu kaɗan kansa kuma ƙasa babu me iya fuskan tan ainihin yanayin da yake a ciki.  Wani murmushi wanda ake ƙira da kafi kuka ciwo ta saki tare da buɗe baki da niyar magana taji tamkar an buga mata abu a tsakar kanta itama hakan ya tilas ta mata haɗiye maganar makut babu shiri tana riƙe kanta ƙam ƙam. Cikin abinda bai wuce kiɓtar idanu ba Sameer Jedaans Turaki yai hanzarin taro matarsa zuwa jikinsa tana karasa somewar jikinsa. Yana saurin nufar emagency room yay da ita. Dagowa Mohan Sameer Jedaans yayi tare da buɗe idanunsa don shima se sannan yasan ko me ke faruwa da Rayuwar Meesherh ɗin tasu. Bayan Mahaifinsa yabi da kallo zuciyarsa shima najin yadda tazo ta toƙare nashi ƙirjin.
    Maah kuwa  daga nan bata sake sanin abinda ya sake faruwa a wurin ba, se buɗe ido tai ta ganta kwance itama a nata gadon majinyatan cikin Hospital ɗin.
 
 "Mohan?" Sheikh juraij ya ambata sunan ganin yadda idanunsa ke janyewa shima. "Are You okay?" Ya sake tambayansa se kuma hankalin kowa ya sake tashi ganin uku na ƙoƙarin haɗuwa duk ata dalilin numfashin mutum ɗaya rakkk. "Don ALLAH Yaya Mohan ka daure ka jure kaima karka kwanta wllh³ kana kwanciya nima duk binku zanyi.d0n ALLAH ka tsaya mu rarrashi juna" Ummeterh ta fadi tana rushewa da wani sabon kukan me ƙarfin daya san Sheikh juraij barin wurin a sukwane yana wani kalan fesar da numfashi. Tallafar kanta Mohan Sameer yayi yana ɗan bubbuga bayanta yana me kasa yin  magana ko yaya ce.
     
  *Maah*
Bayan ta farka   A loƙacin ne  ta  sake gaskata ma  al'amarin lallai ƙaddara ba ta kaɗan ba ta  yawa. In banda haka wllh da tasan abinda zai faru da ɗiyar tan kenan kara ita tayi zamanta gida ita kuma ta fita abinda ya faru da Meesherh ɗin ya faru da ita in yaso.  Sadda kanta kawai ƙasa tayi not saying anything saidai ita kaɗai tasan irin halin da zuciyarta take ciki da irin ɗacin dake danƙare acen cikin tsakiyar ƙirjinta. A hankali ta maida idanunta ta lumshe tana haɗiye yawun daya tokare makoshinta, zafi mai mugun raɗaɗi na dada game ilahirin  zuciyarta ji take kamar ana gasa mata zuciyar a oven ne kuma a  tarfa mata narkakkan dalma.
  Hakan ya sanya Ahankali ta shiga  ɗan bugun gadon da take kwance akai comfortably tana streaming ya Rayuwar Meesherh ɗin ta zata kaya a kuma wanne mataki take zuwa yanzun?
 Prof Sameer Jedaans da ya zuba mata idanunsa tun farkon farkawan nata ne  ya ɗan shiga takawa zuwa a hankali zuwa gareta jiki a matuƙar sanyaya.
  "Ita ƙaddara  da kike kalla  Haleematu  wajibatunce akan ko wane bawa kuma mumini mai iman!. Domin Allah baya taɓa ɗorama bawansa ƙaddarar da bazai iya ɗauka ba. Kana ita ƙaddara da kike gani rubutacciya ce tun daga rabbis samawati! Me yasa kike ma taki ƙaddarar kuka?. Idan muka sanya ma zuciyarsa mu nasibi da miƙa al'amurra ga Sarkin da shi kaɗai ne buwayi gagara misali sai kiga ya sauƙaƙa mana tarin ƙalubalen dake cikin makauniyar ƙaddarar SAMEESHERH. Ya kuma kawo mana mafita akan lamarin da take ciki,Kuma bawai sun ce lalli kwakwalwanta ya gama samun matsala bane suna hasashene akan hakan shi yasa ma suka bada izinin saka hannu domin sake binciko ainihin abinda ke faruwa da ita , kanta ne ya bugu sosai inda har suke hasashen kwakwalwanta ya haɗu da jini. Marar ta kuma wani abu ne daga cikin karafen motan ya daki wurin tare da hudawa ya faso..... Ki daure ki daure ki daure kiyi haƙuri Hali dubu na manzon Allah" 
     Itakam tama gaza yin maganan se kawai wani kalan shiru da tayi ga idanunta da suka janye farat ɗaya.

*All-day diner*
    
Tsaye yake se famar kaiwa da koma yake wani irin zafi na sukarar zuciyarsa, Yasan tunda yaji shiru ba'a haskaka labaran mutuwar Meesherh ba to kuwa tabbas da sauran ranta kenan bata mutu ba. Amma a yadda ya ganta ɗin a dagargaje be ɗauka cewan zata kawo har yanzun bata mutu ba.kasancewan shi ma yana ɗaya daga cikin mutanen da suka je a matsayin ƴan kallo, Shi ya hana kowa ma tunkarar inda take a cewarsa har se hukuma tazo wurin. kuma akan idon sane Sheikh juraij ya ɗauka Meesherh'n jikinta na yalalar da jini sosai. Zuciyan sa yaji ta sake kunbura da ɓacin ran da yake ciki. Sama sama cikin sautin ban haƙuri ya fara jiyo muryan virus yana me bashi haƙuri akan wllh sunyi iyakar yinsu kumama a yadda dai jikinta yake gaskiya be zama lallai Meesherh ɗin ta kai labari. Hakan ne ya sanya shi juyowa a mugun fusace tare da wanke fuskar virus ɗin da wani shegen azababben mari na tashin hankali tare da furta.
  "Will you shut that your dirty mouth up!, are you stupid Ina magana kana yimin zancen banza da wofi, kodai ka sama taɓin hankaline ban sani ba ko bana da labari Impossible wannan abu  bamai yiyuwa bane.... to ni a yanzu babu abinda nake da muradin ji da gani illa naga ana haskaka labaran Mutuwar Meesherh Sameer Jedaans a ko ina a kowacce kafa ta sada zumunta" 
  Yadda ya hayayyaƙo musu ɗin  yana masifar kadai zai shaida cewan lalle a mugun fusace yake.
   "Boss  ka yarda damu na rantse da uban daya haifi ubana seda mukayi raga raga da motar yarin yar nan kuma wllh wllh be zama lallai ta kai labari ba, idan kuwa takai to tabbas ba zata warke garau kamar yadda take a da ɗin taba"
 Kaifi yayi maganan a wannan karon yana wani zazzaro jajayen idanuwansa irin na shegu. "Stop your dirty mouth all over again" daga haka ya fice a room ɗin da suke ciki kai tsaye yana nufar gaban Motarsa dayay Parking nata. Kai tsaye woyansa ya fiddo bayan ya tada motar tare da dokama lambar Moh Ameern Saif ƙira. "Hello Moh Ameern yanzu nake tsintar wani labari wai Diamond Meesherh Jadeens ce ta sama accident right?" Daga ta can ɓangaren me ƙiran kuwa ɗan lumshe ido Moh yayi duk jikinsa sanyaye babu daɗi ya furta. "Wllh Meesherh tana cikin wani yanayi sosai director muhsin se dai a taya mu da adda'a" yana katse ƙiran ya fesar da wani zazzafan numfashin da ya sanya Mohan Sameer Jedaans dubansa yana tambayar ko lafiya? Amsa ya bashi kan abinda Muhsin ɗin ya tambaye shi. Chakkk Sheikh juraij thabit dake woya ya tsaya chakkk na wosu mintinan, kafin yay saurin ɗauke kansa daga garesu jin tambayar da Mohan Sameer ya cillama Moh Ameern ɗin. "Amma daamaki taya maganar da bata fita ba har shi yasan abinda ke faruwa da Meesherh ɗin?" Panping hand nashi yay irin oho ɗin nan.☆ 

☆Umarnin kowa ya tafi haka nan Sheikh juraij ya bayar ganin har zuwa loƙacin bata farfaɗo ba wanda kuma dama can basu sanya Rai da hakan ba kodan a yanayin da take a ciki, Domin maƙil suka cika Hospital ɗin da duka ajalinsa babu wanda be zoba har na nesa kuwa masu aure a garuruwa daban daban amma banda Anniey. Bisa dole don sun nan ba'a wasa da umarnin Sheikh ɗin ya sanya wasu tafiya wasu kuwa sunja sun kafe kan dole suna nan kodan shige da ficen su Maah ɗin. Haka aka ɗin ga ɗibansu a lafiyayyun manyan motoci ana tafiya dasu can Jedaans Mention ɗin. 
   A fusace thabit Jedaans daddy'n juraij ya shigo gidan zaune ya tarar da Madam anniey da kayan maƙulashe gabanta cikin shiga ta alfarma. "Yanzu ke A'isha abinda kike aikatawa kina kallon ya kamata kenan? Ke kuwa me wannan yarinya da uwarta suka aikata miki da zafi har haka? Koma dai miye jinya ta kori komi da kike ji da gani Wllh. Domin ita gata can bamuma tunanin zata rayu amma ki kasa sanya ƙafarki asibitin wllh rashin imanin ki kullum sake gaba gaba yake A'isha na rasa wacce kalan zuciya garek. ki sani Allah (S.W.T) da kansa yace karmu tsani kowa a Rayuwa" Zamanta ta gyara sosai tare da fuskantar mijin nata kana ta furta " Amma ai yace mu naisanta kammu ga waɗan da zasuci zarafinmu ko mutuncinmu, kaga kenan bana da laifi akan hakan ko?kuma shi da kansa yace kada ka damu da wanda bai damu dakai ba,kana yace  kada ka matsanta son wanda baya sonka!! Shi kenan dan banje asibitin suba kenan nayi laifi? Sanin kanka ne ba ƙaunata Haleema takeyi dan me ni  zan kaunaceta  duniya ai cewa tai kaso duk wanda ya nuna maka ƙauna ka nesanci duk  wanda baya ƙaunar ka amma ka  kaucewa nuna wanda baya sonka ƙauna ta hanyar shishshigi dan banyi hakan ba shikenan nayi laifi" tana gama faɗin hakan tayi tafiyarta a wurin. 
   Wata ƙatuwar ajiyar zuciya ya sauƙe shima yana nufar bangarensa yana jin sammm babu daɗi hakan har can kasan zuciyarsa. Daga haka kowa ya kwanta wasu zuciyoyin duk babu daɗi da masu akasin hakan. Wanda tun a daren jiyan suke amsan ƙorafe ƙorafe daga jama'a da dama kan yadda film ɗin be fita ba kana  kwata kwata be kuma fidda wota ma'anar komi ba. Wanda dama tun daga tallan Film ɗin wasu suka fara ƙorafin a sanya musu cikakkiyar jarumarsu wato 𝘿𝙄𝘼𝙈𝙊𝙉  Ꮇ𝞔𝞔𝘚Η𝞔Ʀh    𝕁𝔼ᴰᴬᴬᴺˢ, wanda ata sanadiyyar hakan ne komi daya faru da ita ya farun.     Kacaniyace me ƙarfin gaske ta sake ɓallewa wanda ya sanya har wasu daga cikin tsirarin mutanen kewaye Industry ɗin. Dalili hakan ya sake jefa zuciyar muhsin Aliey cikin kunci da kuma hasalashi. Cikin abinda bai wuce 20 minutes ba sega wata rantsatstsar black RR car nan ta shigo cikin wurin. Wani irin harbawa taji zuciyanta yayi a take  hakan yasa bama tasan lokacin data lula Duniyar tunani ba, Sai kawai wannan unique ƙamshin da tun garkon haɗuwansu na farko ta gaza mance kalansa  ba ne ya bugo yana kawo mata ziyararn kai tsaye.  ɗago idanuwanta masu kyau tai tana kallonsa. Sanye yake da Ash balmains na wata crazy shirt me shegiyar tsada da straight wando sai tsadaddun cover  shoes ɗin  dake ƙafarnsa na ballies wanda kodan yawan sanyasu ɗin da yake ne ya sa kai tsaye zaka gane cewan shi ɗin soldier ne. ɗauke kanta take son yi daga face nashi, sedai ta gaza hakan don wani irin sabon kyaun da take daɗa iya kalla me kyau, wanda kyaun yafi yanayi da irin  morning beauty ɗin nan ko ko dai muce morning freshness. Duk yacce taso ɗauke kanta daga kallonsa gaza hakan tayi domin kuwa  fin ƙarfinta  zuciyarta tai ta kasa ɗauke fararen idanuwanta akansa tana masa wani irin kallan da ita kanta bama tasan tana masa ba. Shi kuwa tunda yay tozali da kyakkyawan face nata  tsananin azabtuwan da zuciyarsa yay akan sonta da yaƙi barin Heart nashi ne  ya sa ya kasa ɗauke idanuwansa daga gareta harya yana ƙoƙarin  loosing control nashi gurin bayyanar da hakan har idanuwansa nason cikowa da wani abu da yafi kama  da hawaye kai tsaye. Fitowan Aunty Ummeterh daga cikin mall ɗin ne ya katse shi daga buɗe bakin dayay da niyan mata magana ita kuwa Aunty Ummeterh'n ba tare da tama lura da wanda ke tsayen ba ne ya sanya  tace "I'm so sorry bestie mun tsaida k......
   Se dai bakinta yay shiru ganin wani a tare da Meesherh ɗin, hakan ya sanya ta naɗe sauran maganan datay niyan yi ɗin kuwa.   *Sheikh juraij*
Farkawan kenan  daga baccin bayan la'asar ɗin data konta hakan ya sanya ta tashi a ɗan hanzarce don sanin yanzu duk inda yake ya dawo. Duka ɗakunan gidan ta shiga dubawa sedai babu shi hakan ya sanya ta leƙa compound, sesai ganin car nashi daya fita da itane ya sanya ta sake komawa tama sake dudduba sauran ɗakunan. Da kyar ta sama ganinsa a cikin wani Master bedroom nashi tayi masararn samun shi wanda yake kwance kan tsakiyar Raoun bed nashi me tsananin laushin da kai tsaye idan mutum ya kwanta yake yin luf ya lafe cikin bed ɗin. Kana sanya ƙafarka kuwa a cikin parlour'n  abu na farko da  zai fara  maka maraba, Shi ne sassanyan ƙamshin turaren da humidifier ke bayarwa. Wani gajeren numfashi Samha ta ja yana nufar kan bed ɗin.  Har zata tashe shi se kuma ta tuna da ya hana mata tashinsa  se kawai ta juya bayan ta gama bin jikinsa  da mayen kallon da bata sama gani ba dake lulluɓe da dubet tabar room ɗin. Shi kuwa ashe duk abinda take yana jinta  kawai ya sama kan sane a wani yanayi na daban wanda tun jiya  Meesherh taƙi barin tunani da kuma kansa. Inda yaki wani mayen feelings nata kamar ze kashe shi amma ya danne hakan. So da dama har gani yake kamar shiya ɗorama kansa hakan na rashin feelings ko wacce macen dake duniya se ita kaɗai. Gani yake kamar ALLAH se iya tashinsa da laifi akan hakan, wanda a daren jiya bayan ya dawo shi da kansa yake har room nata da niyar kasancewa da ita tunda yaji feelings nashi ya motsa. Sedai tamkar abin asiri yana shiga room ɗin nata kallo ɗaya kawai ya mata yaji farar ɗaya feelings ɗin na ɓacewa ɓatt. Wanda hakan yaso tashin hankalin sa sosai domin yana tsoron ALLAH ya kamashi da hakkin Samha a kan sa,  Shi ne abinda ya hana shi fita duk a ranar yau ɗin. Kuma da ko tuna sunan Meesherl ɗin yayi se yaji abun ya motsa masa kamar ya karshi. Bayan fitan ta da kamar 15 minutes ya miƙe  a natse bada garaje ba yana faɗawa cikin toilet.
    
Kayan dake jikinsa ya zare lafiyayyar farar fatarsa na bayyana tana ɗaukan ido saboda samun hutu da tsadaddiyar gyara da original skin products masu tsada da kyau da yake amfani dasu. shower ya sakarma kansa ruwan dumi yana rufe idanuwansa ruwan na sauƙa akan fuskansa suna ganganrawa jikinsa suna dumama jikinsa a hankali.
 Ya jima sosai  sosai  yana wankan  kafin ya fito ɗaure  da babban towel me kauri brown jikinsa na fidda ƙamshin shower Soap ɗinsa na Ocean seaweed. Da Mini  clean blue towel  ya shiga tsane jikinsa a natse.  kafin ya shiga  shafe jikinsa da bodymilk da mix na oils na diamon dia da suke saka jikinsa glowing da kyan gani da wani irin soft ya shafa perfs dasu bodyMist da sauransu, kafin ya gyara fuskansa yai brushing gashin kansa dayake a kwance da santsi irin na asalin ustazan larabawa.
   Bai woni ɗauki loƙaci ba sosai  ya shirya kansa cikin wani brawn cashmere daya nutsu a jikinsa saboda  tasan kuɗin da ya lasa. A cikin tarin tsadaddun designer agogayensa dake jere a doguwar glass drawer'n  ya buɗe yana ɗaukan ɗaya ya saka  a hannunsa da gargasa ke kwance luf luf gwanin birgewa.
  A natse ya fito shirye fess zuwa ainihin parlour'n sa da ko ina a parlour'n yake neat ba hayaniyar komai a parlon komai  is so gentle unique simple and luxuries ne kawai ke magana a parlour'n kawai. A babban parlour'n ya yada zango yana ɗaukan ipad nashi ya shiga duba jerin schedule ɗin da aka turo masa ta cikin Gmail nashi. Anan yaci karo da bayanan da ya bada cikin sirru a duba masa dangane da accident ɗin daya faru da Meesherh.  Duba komai yayi zuciyan sa na ƙona ganin  sunan Moh Ameern dumu dumu a cikin jerin list na wanda aka lissafo masu hannu a cikin case na accident ɗin. 
    Idanunsa yaja yana lumshewa abinda ke ƙoƙarin taso masa yana maidasa da ƙarfin adda'a.

Kwanci tashi akace asaran me rai haka Ahmed xm ya dinga bibiyan Meesherh har seda ta fara kulashi, wanda cikin ƙanƙanin lokaci wata soyayya me zafi ta shiga tsakaninsu su. Amma yasha fama tabbas ba laifi don wai duk dan ya guje mata har Matsalan ta da In banda su Family'n babu wanda ya sani seda ta faɗin meshi cewan barata taɓa haihuwa ba. Amma amsan daya bata shi ne dalilin daya sanya kai tsaye ita kuma ta buɗe masa zuciyan ta. "Meesherh be wai haihuwanki ko rashin haihuwan ki nake SO ba ke zatinki nake SO har cikin zuciya da ruhina kuma na yarda in aure ki a hakan in yasa mu mutu hakan ba tare da tunanin samun haihuwa ba Cwty..."
 Wannan maganan nashi dama wanda yakan faɗin mata makamantansu sune suka rinjayeta a kansa. Wanda yakai a yanzu har gidama amsan da maganar Ahmed xm ɗin.
    An kuma saki Film nata a duniya wanda yaja wani irin ƙura a duniya don kuwa duk inda ka gilma maganar Film ɗin ake wanda ya fita da wani irin zafi na garari. A wannan karon kamm har gida Jedaans Aysha suka zo suka kawoma Babbar Role model nasu ziyara. Inda suma suka zama manyan yara sosai wanda a yanzu suke yawan ganin Meesherh ɗin akai akai. Suma kansu sunan su wani irin fice yay a duniyar gajimare. Wanda suke nasu video's ɗin suna ɗaurawa amma ba wanda zeja magana ba masu nutsuwa suke yi cikin aji. Kowa yana da salon nashi masoyan wanda idan ka leƙa shafin Aysha se kaga kamar tafi Rushuna person sunan, itan ma da ka shiga shafin Rushuna person ɗin saka kalla tamkar ma tafi Aysha ɗin suna. Wannan abu ma daɗa Hargitsa masoya mabiyansa.

   Kasancewar shirye shiryen azuma ya gabato ya sanya uncle El-muaz da Uncle jamaal Jedaans dawowa Nigeria wanda duk shekararn duniya haka suke a gida suke azumi da kuma sallah.  Inda dawowan nasu shi ne ya zama mabuɗin komai cikin sauri amma ba irin na garajeba. Dan kai tsaye a cikin ƴan tsakanin Awwb El-muaz Jedaans ya bayyana soyayyar sa ga Meesherh wanda dama sosai yake sonta amma ganin kamar da wota alaƙarn soyayya tsakanin ta da  Sheikh juraij ne ya sanya shi kin bayyana mata love nashi. Amma da suka zo wannan zuwan se kawai ya buɗe mata zuciyan sa wanda ita kuma a lokacin da riga ta kamu da love ɗin Ahmed xm. Amma da akace jininka duk inda yake jinin kane se cikin ƙanƙanin lokaci ya rinjayeta akan soyayyar sa me tsafta da yake mata. Wanda tama iyayen dadi dan kuwa dama kowannensu auren Meesherh ɗin yake da burin ganin. Se dai wani matsalan da kai tsaye aka samu shi ne mahaifiyan Awwb ɗin Mum Khadija don kai tsaye tace bata yarda ba ba kuma da yawon taba. Da me yasa tun a azziƙi basu bashi auren Meesherh ɗin ba har se data sama matsala akan haihuwa a  sannan ne kuma za'a liƙa ma ɗan ta to wllh bata yarda ba.
   Shi kuma El-muaz Jedaans Turaki ya ce mazawar ta hana auren Awwb El-muaz Jedaans da Meesherh Sameer Jadeens to taje itama a bakin nata auren. Sosai hankalin mum Khadija yake a tashe  wanda take cike da tsabar baƙin ciki da takaicin zuwansun Nigeria'n kasancewan ita ƴer cen Islamabad ɗin ce.  Alhamdulillh kuma haka aka shiga watan azumi lafiya cikin salama da buwayer Ubangijin al'arshi. Wanda kuma kai tsaye aka tsaida maganar auren bayan salla da sati ɗaya, ita kanta Maah bawai tana son auren ba har ma se tabi jin kwara Ahmed xm ɗin akan duk Family'n JEDAANS ɗin.  ALLAH Sarki shi kuwa bawan Allah yana can bama ya kasar yana cen ƙasar saudia. besan wainan da ake toyawa ba. Dake komai na alaƙansu bawai sauyawa yayiba komai na alaƙansu nanan sedai ta gaza sanar masa da abinda yake faruwa domin wani mugun tausayi taji yana bata.
   Haka kwanakin azumin ke tafiya ayi ba ayi ba har kaci karfin watan azumin wanda mafi yawan dake ribanta da irin yadda wannan shahararren malamin Sheikh juraij thabit Jedaans duk wani masoyi bemin fejin nashi dama wanda suke samun tafsr nashi basu son watan azumin ya ƙare.  Domin wata irin nutsuwa ne ke zaunar musu da kuma karuwa daga irin yadda yake farke gaskiya akan komai ya kuma dilmiya masoyansa ji da son kasancewan Ina sune shi. Dan kallon ɗan baiwa haihuwan Sa'a kawai suke mai.inda iyaka aikin ladan da yake bazama jama'a kaɗai suke ƙara rikicewa akansa da ƙaunar sa. Banda gidajen marayu gidajen marasa ƙarfi kai dama kowa da kowa. Dan kawai ribata da watan me tarin falala da tarin alkairuka. Kuma har zuwa wannan lokacin babu abinda ya shiga tsakaninsa da Samha wanda a cikin watan azumin har kukansa yakai wa mahaliccin sa akan ya bashi feelings ɗin da zai iya fuskantan matarshi Samha ɗin.  Kuma yanzu sosai yake ƙoƙarin ganin ya faranta mata  kuma ƙoƙarin janyo ta jikinsa wanda wani bin har kwana sukanyi tare amma ba tare data taɓa kallon ko satin cibiyoyin sa bane bare kuma jikinsa. Ita kanta yanzu duk wani rawan kai nata se ta nema shi ta rasa  ga uwarta data sako ta gaba akan lallai tasan duk yacce zatai tayi ta sama ciki ko cikin watan azumin nan ne.  Sedai takai ta kawo woyar mahaifiyanta ma ta dena dauka a takaice don wllh ita tama fara gajiyawa. Sema kawai taji auren Sheikh din na fita a kanta. Wanda take jin tana gan da zuwa ta bayyana ma iyayensa cewan shi ɗin ba cikakken namiji bane in yaso komai ze faru ya faru. Se shima sannu a hankali ta fara janye masa wanda koba komi se hakan ya masa daɗi.
  Gefe guda kuma har yanzun yana nan akan case na accident ɗin Meesherh,wanda ya gama kammala komai akan case ɗin bayan sallar yake jira akan ya dokasu a kotu. A ranar da aka kai azumi na ashirin cif a ranar ne Prof Sameer Jedaans ya  bama Sheikh juraij waliyyin da zai ɗaurama Meesherh Jadeens ɗin aure.
   Wanda iyaka waliyyin da aka bashi Anniey ta ringa surfa balai da masifan hakan har seda itama daddy ya taka mata birki kana ta shiga hankalinta amma kuma bawai ta haƙura tana ji tana gani a kaƙaba ma danta waliyyin auren Meesherh ɗin bane........
